-- | Shared independent media, encoded-word, callback and registry fixture consumers.
module Main where
  import Text.Mime
  import Effect.Mime
  import Effect.Stream
  import Data.Bool
  import Data.Bytes
  import Data.List
  import Data.Machine
  import Data.Nat
  import Data.Option
  import Data.Result
  import Data.String (String, string-to-bytes, string-from-bytes, empty-string)

  -- | Compare byte contents without a global mutable-buffer equality instance.
  same :: Bytes -> Bytes -> Bool
  same first last = eq-i64 (bytes-compare first last) 0

  -- | Compare sorted exact key/value observations.
  same-pairs :: List (Bytes, Bytes) -> List (Bytes, Bytes) -> Bool
  same-pairs actual expected = case actual of
    Nil -> case expected of Nil -> True; _ -> False
    Cons pair rest -> case expected of
      Nil -> False
      Cons other tail -> case pair of
        (key, value) -> case other of
          (expectedKey, expectedValue) -> same key expectedKey && same value expectedValue && same-pairs rest tail

  -- | Validate normalized base, nil-map distinction, exact parameters and error text.
  media-is :: Bytes -> Bytes -> Bool -> List (Bytes, Bytes) -> String -> Bool
  media-is input base absent pairs message =
    let result = mime-parse-media-type input in
    same (string-to-bytes (mime-media-base result)) base &&
      (case mime-media-error result of None -> message == ""; Some error -> mime-error-message error == message) &&
      (case mime-media-parameters result of None -> absent; Some params -> not absent && same-pairs (mime-parameter-pairs params) pairs)

  -- | Validate deterministic original-key ordering and exact parameter serialization.
  format-is :: Bytes -> List (Bytes, Bytes) -> Bytes -> Bool
  format-is base pairs expected = same (string-to-bytes (mime-format-media-type base (mime-parameters pairs))) expected

  -- | Validate every raw selector and exact charset spelling through the public encoder.
  encode-is :: U8 -> Bytes -> Bytes -> Bytes -> Bool
  encode-is selector charset input expected = same (mime-word-encode-bytes (MimeWordEncoder selector) charset input) expected

  -- | Arbitrary caller error values retain both a numeric identity and an immutable message.
  data FixtureError where
    -- | Distinguish callback creation from converted-reader failures without string-only narrowing.
    FixtureError :: Nat -> String -> FixtureError

  -- | Render only fixture errors while preserving and checking their identity fields.
  fixture-error-message :: FixtureError -> String
  fixture-error-message error = case error of
    FixtureError identity message -> if (identity == 47 && message == "fixture charset callback failure") || (identity == 83 && message == "fixture converted-reader failure") then message else "unexpected fixture error identity"

  -- | Preserve decode error categories rather than flattening callback values prematurely.
  decode-message :: MimeDecodeFailure FixtureError -> String
  decode-message error = case error of
    MimeWordSyntaxFailure failure -> mime-error-message failure
    MimeCharsetCallbackFailure failure -> fixture-error-message failure
    MimeCharsetReadFailure failure -> fixture-error-message failure
    MimeCharsetInvalidCount requested actual -> "invalid converted count"
    MimeOutputWriteFailure failure -> case failure of StreamClosed text -> text; _ -> "unexpected output failure"

  -- | Reset serialized trace/sink I/O state without retaining any language pointers.
  foreign import ccall "mime_fixture_reset" raw-reset :: forall r. I64 ->{IO r} I64
  -- | Record one callback label and its decoded input, assigning a stable ordinal.
  foreign import ccall "mime_fixture_event" raw-event :: forall r. I64 -> I64 -> I64 -> I64 ->{IO r} I64
  -- | Record one pull-read request and simultaneous prefix/status.
  foreign import ccall "mime_fixture_read" raw-read :: forall r. I64 -> I64 -> I64 -> I64 -> I64 ->{IO r} I64
  -- | Record an optional whole-copy operation and its exact result/error.
  foreign import ccall "mime_fixture_copy" raw-copy :: forall r. I64 -> I64 -> I64 -> I64 ->{IO r} I64
  -- | Compare all callback observations against an independent trace digest.
  foreign import ccall "mime_fixture_digest" raw-digest :: forall r. I64 ->{IO r} U64
  -- | Write an exact prefix to an independently observable failing sink.
  foreign import ccall "mime_fixture_write" raw-write :: forall r. I64 -> I64 ->{IO r} I64
  -- | Observe all confirmed output bytes after success or failure.
  foreign import ccall "mime_fixture_sink_size" raw-sink-size :: forall r. I64 ->{IO r} I64
  -- | Observe one initialized external sink byte.
  foreign import ccall "mime_fixture_sink_byte" raw-sink-byte :: forall r. I64 ->{IO r} U8
  -- | Execute the independent scalar oracle against the actual vector-class and copy objects.
  foreign import ccall "mime_fixture_kernel_check" raw-kernel-check :: forall r. I64 ->{IO r} I32

  -- | Retain a borrowed byte owner until one external call completes.
  hold :: forall a. Bytes -> a -> a
  hold bytes result = case bytes-len bytes of _ -> result

  -- | Copy a checked fixture interval into independent storage.
  part :: Bytes -> Nat -> Nat -> Bytes
  part input at count = if at + count <= bytes-len input then
    let out = bytes-new count in let copied = bytes-copy-range out input 0 at count in out
    else bytes-new 0

  -- | Record a result before its independent output buffer can be released.
  trace-read :: forall r. using (io :: Io r) => I64 -> Nat -> Bytes -> I64 ->{IO r} Unit
  trace-read identity request bytes status =
    if request <= 9223372036854775807 then
      let recorded = hold bytes (raw-read identity (nat-to-i64 request) (bytes-data bytes) (nat-to-i64 (bytes-len bytes)) status) in ()
    else ()

  -- | A pull reader with exact two-byte chunks and independently injected terminal behavior.
  traced-reader :: forall r. using (io :: Io r) => Bytes -> Nat -> I64 -> String -> Bool -> MimeCharsetReader r FixtureError
  traced-reader input at identity policy gap = mime-charset-reader (\request ->
    if policy == "empty-more" && not gap then
      let empty = bytes-new 0 in let recorded = trace-read identity request empty 0 in
      ((empty, MimeCharsetMore), traced-reader input at identity policy True)
    else if at < bytes-len input then
      let remaining = bytes-len input - at in
      let short = if request < 2 then request else 2 in
      let count = if short < remaining then short else remaining in
      let out = part input at count in
      let failed = policy == "prefix-error" && at + count == bytes-len input in
      let ended = policy == "prefix-eof" && at + count == bytes-len input in
      let recorded = trace-read identity request out (if failed then 2 else if ended then 1 else 0) in
      ((out, if failed then MimeCharsetFailure (FixtureError 83 "fixture converted-reader failure") else if ended then MimeCharsetEnd else MimeCharsetMore), traced-reader input (at + count) identity policy True)
    else
      let empty = bytes-new 0 in
      let failed = policy == "read-error" in
      let recorded = trace-read identity request empty (if failed then 2 else 1) in
      ((empty, if failed then MimeCharsetFailure (FixtureError 83 "fixture converted-reader failure") else MimeCharsetEnd), traced-reader input at identity policy True))

  -- | Optional whole-copy behavior is observed instead of invoking the pull fallback.
  traced-copy :: forall r. using (io :: Io r) => Bytes -> I64 -> String -> Unit ->{IO r} Result FixtureError Bytes
  traced-copy input identity policy unit =
    let failed = policy == "copy-fast-error" in
    let recorded = hold input (raw-copy identity (bytes-data input) (nat-to-i64 (bytes-len input)) (if failed then 2 else 0)) in
    if failed then Err (FixtureError 83 "fixture converted-reader failure") else Ok input

  -- | Callback reads its complete fresh input, records exact lowercase labels, and
  -- exposes an ordinary or complete-copy converted-reader capability.
  charset-callback :: forall r. using (io :: Io r) => String -> String -> ByteReader r ->{IO r} Result FixtureError (MimeCharsetReader r FixtureError)
  charset-callback policy charset source = case stream-read-all 32768 source of
    (Err error, next) -> Err (FixtureError 99 "unexpected callback input failure")
    (Ok input, next) ->
      let label = string-to-bytes charset in
      let identity = hold label (hold input (raw-event (bytes-data label) (nat-to-i64 (bytes-len label)) (bytes-data input) (nat-to-i64 (bytes-len input)))) in
      if policy == "error" || policy == "both-error" then Err (FixtureError 47 "fixture charset callback failure")
      else
        let bytes = if policy == "invalid-utf8" then bytes-from-list (Cons 255 (Cons 254 (Cons 0 Nil))) else input in
        let reader = traced-reader bytes 0 identity policy False in
        if policy == "copy-fast" || policy == "copy-fast-error" then Ok (mime-charset-reader-with-copy (traced-copy bytes identity policy) reader) else Ok reader

  -- | Inspect and replace the callback field without invoking it or losing its caller error type.
  decoder-for :: forall r. using (io :: Io r) => String -> MimeWordDecoder r FixtureError
  decoder-for policy =
    let initial = mime-word-decoder (if policy == "none" then None else Some (charset-callback policy)) in
    mime-word-decoder-with-charset-reader (mime-word-decoder-charset-reader initial) (mime-word-decoder None)

  -- | Validate complete returned bytes/error plus all callback/read/copy observations.
  decode-is :: forall r. using (io :: Io r) => Bool -> Bytes -> String -> Bytes -> String -> U64 ->{IO r} Bool
  decode-is header input policy expected message trace =
    let reset = raw-reset (-1) in
    let decoder = decoder-for policy in
    let result = if header then mime-header-decode decoder input else mime-word-decode decoder input in
    (case result of Ok out -> message == "" && same out expected; Err error -> bytes-len expected == 0 && decode-message error == message) && eq-u64 (raw-digest 0) trace

  -- | Compare a fresh list of sorted extension buffers.
  same-list :: List Bytes -> List Bytes -> Bool
  same-list first last = case first of
    Nil -> case last of Nil -> True; _ -> False
    Cons a rest -> case last of Nil -> False; Cons b tail -> same a b && same-list rest tail

  -- | A descriptor-like fixture sink with externally observable advanced progress.
  fixture-sink :: forall r. using (io :: Io r) => ByteWriter r
  fixture-sink = stream-writer (\input ->
    let count = hold input (raw-write (bytes-data input) (nat-to-i64 (bytes-len input))) in
    if count >= 0 then (Ok (nat-from-i64 count), fixture-sink)
    else (Err (StreamClosed "fixture sink failure"), fixture-sink))

  -- | Compare only initialized sink bytes after an exact length observation.
  sink-bytes :: forall r. using (io :: Io r) => Bytes -> Nat ->{IO r} Bool
  sink-bytes expected at = if at < bytes-len expected then
    u8-to-nat (raw-sink-byte (nat-to-i64 at)) == bytes-get expected at && sink-bytes expected (at + 1)
    else True

  -- | Check complete confirmed output rather than treating failure as zero progress.
  sink-is :: forall r. using (io :: Io r) => Bytes ->{IO r} Bool
  sink-is expected = eq-i64 (raw-sink-size 0) (nat-to-i64 (bytes-len expected)) && sink-bytes expected 0

  -- | Preserve the concrete sink failure vocabulary in short-write controls.
  stream-message :: StreamError -> String
  stream-message error = case error of StreamClosed text -> text; _ -> "unexpected stream failure"

  -- | Check one complete encoded output against a compliant failing sink budget.
  write-is :: forall r. using (io :: Io r) => U8 -> Bytes -> Bytes -> I64 -> Bytes -> String ->{IO r} Bool
  write-is selector charset input budget expected message =
    let reset = raw-reset budget in
    case mime-word-write (MimeWordEncoder selector) charset input fixture-sink of
      (Ok count, next) -> message == "" && count == bytes-len expected && sink-is expected
      (Err error, next) -> stream-message error == message && sink-is expected

  -- | Registration validates atomically and preserves the caller's original context.
  registry-add-is :: Bytes -> Bytes -> String -> MimeRegistry -> (Bool, MimeRegistry)
  registry-add-is extension media message registry = case mime-registry-add extension media registry of
    Ok next -> (message == "", next)
    Err error -> (mime-error-message error == message, registry)

  -- | Extension queries retain all parse failures while omitting nonsemantic parameters.
  registry-extensions-is :: Bytes -> List Bytes -> String -> MimeRegistry -> Bool
  registry-extensions-is media expected message registry = case mime-registry-extensions media registry of
    Ok values -> message == "" && same-list values expected
    Err error -> mime-error-message error == message

  -- | Decode before writing, preserving an untouched sink when charset conversion fails.
  header-write-is :: forall r. using (io :: Io r) => Bytes -> I64 -> Bytes -> String ->{IO r} Bool
  header-write-is input budget expected message =
    let reset = raw-reset budget in
    case mime-header-write (decoder-for "none") input fixture-sink of
      (Ok count, next) -> message == "" && count == bytes-len expected && sink-is expected
      (Err error, next) -> decode-message error == message && sink-is expected

  -- | Validate independent encoded case 512.
  fixture-aa :: forall r. using (io :: Io r) => Unit ->{IO r} Bool
  fixture-aa unit = encode-is 98 (bytes-from-list (Cons 255 Nil)) (string-to-bytes "aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaé") (bytes-from-list (Cons 61 (Cons 63 (Cons 255 (Cons 63 (Cons 98 (Cons 63 (Cons 89 (Cons 87 (Cons 70 (Cons 104 (Cons 89 (Cons 87 (Cons 70 (Cons 104 (Cons 89 (Cons 87 (Cons 70 (Cons 104 (Cons 89 (Cons 87 (Cons 70 (Cons 104 (Cons 89 (Cons 87 (Cons 70 (Cons 104 (Cons 89 (Cons 87 (Cons 72 (Cons 68 (Cons 113 (Cons 81 (Cons 61 (Cons 61 (Cons 63 (Cons 61 Nil)))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))))
